- **update-todo**: Write command. TC's `PATCH` changes only the fields sent. An input left out or `""` is not a change; an explicit `null` clears `due-date` and empties `assignees` (`title`, `description` and `label` cannot be cleared — TC keeps a label once set). A due date ending in `Z` is sent as `+0000`, which is the form TC accepts. The Core definition declares no `If-Match` for ToDos. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/construction/trimble-connect/commands/update-todo.md) · ★7)
